Precompute function `build_info(n)`:
```
def build_info(n):
    info={'n':n, 'ms':[], 'j1v':[], 'j1p':[], 'j2v':[], 'j2p':[], 'diag':[], 'valid':[]}
    start = n-1
    for p in range(n):
        m_min = min(p, n-1-p)
        m_max = max(n-1+p, 2*n-2-p)
        L = m_max - m_min + 1
        m = np.arange(m_min, m_max+1, dtype=int)
        d = m - (n-1)
        j1 = p - d
        mask1 = (j1>=0)&(j1<n)
        pos1 = np.nonzero(mask1)[0]
        j2 = p + d
        mask2 = (j2>=0)&(j2<n)
        pos2 = np.nonzero(mask2)[0]
        diag_rel = np.nonzero(d==0)[0]  # one
        info append (m_min, m_max, pos1, j1[mask1], pos2, j2[mask2], diag_rel[0])
    return info
```
Total memory okay. But building pos arrays for every p: each pos length n, total 2n*n=980k ints. Good.

For p=0, range length 2n-1, pos1 length n (d -n+1..0), pos2 length n (d 0..n-1); one overlap at d=0.

Let's design coordinate_descent with chunk projection:
```
def coordinate_descent(h, n, max_passes, deadline, rng, chunk=25, lam=0.0):
    h = h.copy()
    c = np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
    info = build_coord_info(n)
    best = compute_c5(h,n)
    idx = np.arange(n)
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        rng.shuffle(idx)
        changed = 0
        since_proj = 0
        for p in idx:
            x = solve_one_coord(c,h,info,p,lam)
            if abs(x-h[p])>1e-12:
                delta=x-h[p]
                # update c using info
                m0, m1, pos1,v1,j1? etc
                L = m1-m0+1
                a = zeros? We can reuse from solve? solve allocated a. To avoid recompute, return a? solve could return x,a,m0,m1. But after solve loop, a not modified? We can return a and diag. Simpler recompute a in update; but duplicate work. Better solve_one_coord returns x, a, m0, m1.
                update c
                h[p]=x
                changed +=1
                since_proj +=1
                if since_proj >= chunk:
                    h=project_feasible(h,n)
                    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
                    since_proj=0
        if since_proj>0:
            h=project_feasible(h,n)
            c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')
        cur=compute_c5(h,n)
        if cur<best: best=cur
        if changed==0: break
    return h,best
```
But projection after chunk changes sum exactly; c recomputed. Good. During chunk, sum drift at most chunk*(x-h) maybe manageable. If x changes to boundary, drift maybe up to chunk*1. For chunk=20, small.

def solve_coord(c, h, item):
    p,start,stop,pos1,v1,pos2,v2,diag_rel = item
    L = stop-start
    a=np.zeros(L)
    a[pos1] += 1-h[v1]
    a[pos2] -= h[v2]
    cseg = c[start:stop]
    h0 = h[p]
    unaff = 0.0
    if start>0: unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:start].max()))
    if stop < len(c): unaff = max(unaff, float(c[stop:].max()))
    # line masks excluding diag
    tol=1e-14
    pos = a > tol
    neg = a < -tol
    # exclude diag
    if diag_rel>=0:
        pos[diag_rel]=False
        neg[diag_rel]=False
    zero = (a>=-tol)&(a<=tol)
    if diag_rel>=0: zero[diag_rel]=False
    max_zero = float(cseg[zero].max()) if zero.any() else -1e300
    Llo = max(0.0, unaff, max_zero)
    Lhi = float(c.max())  # global current max at old h0
    if Lhi <= Llo + 1e-13:
        return h0, a
    # diag data

    const_diag = c_diag - h0 + h0*h0
    # Precompute pos/neg indices and a values, maybe c values?
    pos_idx = np.nonzero(pos)[0]
    neg_idx = np.nonzero(neg)[0]
    # If no pos and no neg and no diag? not.
    def feasible? No closures, inline bisection.
```

Project_feasible algorithm: Need robust and fast. Let's design:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
    target = n*0.5
    # If sum close return
    for _ in range(100):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-14: break
            # Add either uniform to room or proportional? If add diff to all room capped: h += min(1, diff/tot)*room may not reduce diff enough if some saturate. Use water fill: step = diff/tot; h = clip(h + step*room,0,1). This distributes proportional to room.
        else:
            tot = h.sum()
            step = diff/tot
            h = clip(h + step*h,0,1)
    # final exact correction
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if diff>1e-14:
       # choose indices with room, add sequentially
       order = np.argsort(1-h)?? We can just loop over all: add = min(diff,1-h[i]); h[i]+=add; diff-=add; if diff<=break
    elif diff<-1e-14:
       ... subtract
    return h
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h.copy(),0,1)
   target=n/2
   for _ in range(100):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      t=diff/n
      h=np.clip(h+t,0,1)
   # residual exact:
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if diff>0:
      # add to variables below 1 in order of highest room? Choose partition
      i=0
      while diff>1e-13 and i<n*10:
         for i in range(n):
            if diff<=0: break
            room=1-h[i]
            add=min(diff,room)
            if add>0:
               h[i]+=add; diff-=add
   elif diff<0:
      i=0
      while diff<-1e-13:
         for i...
            room=h[i]
            sub=min(-diff,room)
```

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h0=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  s0=h0.sum()
  if abs(s0-target)<1e-12: return h0
  lo,hi = -1.0,1.0
  # sum monotonic in t
  for 50 iters:
    mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(h0+mid,0,1).sum()
    if s<target: lo=mid else hi=mid
  h=np.clip(h0+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
  # exact residual with free variables
```

Let's write `build_info(n, orient)`:
```
def build_info(n, orient):
    info=[]
    nm1=n-1; total=2*n-2
    for p in range(n):
       m_min = min(p, nm1 - p)
       m_max = max(nm1 + p, total - p)
       stop=m_max+1; start=m_min
       d = np.arange(start, stop, dtype=np.int64) - nm1
       # orient 0: add p-d, subtract p+d
       if orient == 0:
          j_add = p - d; mask_add=(j_add>=0)&(j_add<n)
          j_sub = p + d; mask_sub=(j_sub>=0)&(j_sub<n)
       else:
          j_add = p + d; mask_add=...
          j_sub = p - d; mask_sub=...
       pos_add = np.nonzero(mask_add)[0].astype(np.int32)
       vals_add = j_add[mask_add].astype(np.int32)
       pos_sub = np.nonzero(mask_sub)[0].astype(np.int32)
       vals_sub = j_sub[mask_sub].astype(np.int32)
       diag_rel = int(np.argmax(d == 0))  # should exist
       info.append((p,start,stop,pos_add,vals_add,pos_sub,vals_sub,diag_rel))
    return info
```
Derivation: coordinate coefficient: add term from factor h_p times (1-h_other) where h_p is first factor in c expression; subtract term where h_p is second factor. Orientation determines whether other index is p-d or p+d for add/sub. For assumed output, orient=0: add p-d, sub p+d. For reversed, orient=1: add p+d, sub p-d. Calibration will choose.

Now, coordinate_descent function signature:
```
def coordinate_descent(h, n, deadline, max_passes, rng, chunk=20, info=None, orient=None):
    if orient is None: orient=get_orientation()
    if info is None: info=build_info(n,orient)
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    idx=np.arange(n)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    for it in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       rng.shuffle(idx)
       changed=0; since_chunk=0
       for p in idx:
          item=info[p]
          x, a = solve_coord(c, h, item)
          delta=x-h[p]
          if abs(delta)>1e-13:
             start,stop=item[1],item[2]
             c[start:stop] += delta*a
             c[start+item[7]] -= delta*delta
             h[p]=x
             changed +=1; since_chunk+=1
             if since_chunk >= chunk:
                h=project_feasible(h,n)
                c=np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')
                since_chunk=0
                cur=compute_c5(h,n)
                if cur<best_c-1e-12:
                    best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
       if since_chunk>0:
          h=project_feasible(h,n)
          c=np.correlate(...)
          cur=compute_c5
          update best
       # after each pass, maybe if no improvement after projection, keep last run longer; if no changed, break
       if changed==0:
          # still evaluate? already
          break
       # if best_c has not improved for many passes? Could stop? But continue maybe.
       elapsed check.
    # final project and compute
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c5=compute_c5(h,n)
    if c5<best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5
    return best_h, best_c
```
Need note: During pass, h after projection within chunk drift. The coordinate solve at start of a chunk uses c from feasible h. Then it applies up to chunk coordinate updates without recompute; c may drift due to incremental exact for each coordinate using old h and previous updates in same chunk? Important: When updating coordinate p, then later coordinate q, the c used includes p update; solve for q computes a based on current h (including p) and c (including p). Good. Update c for q uses a and delta; but q update may affect lags that include p's new value? The coefficient a for q is linear in other h values, and we update c by delta*a where a computed before q update using current h (including p). This is exact for changing q only, because c is multilinear in coordinates except diag. Since after changing p, c is correct. Good. If chunk projection changes many h, recompute c exact. Good.

The incremental update for q uses a computed from h before q; update c[start:stop] += delta*a. But what about diagonal of q: subtract delta^2. The c currently includes q^2; correct.

Now, during bisection inside solve_coord, cseg is a view of c; a computed. The `cseg` values for affected lags include current h. Unaffected c outside. Good.

Potential issue: `c[start+diag_rel] -= delta*delta` after `c[start:stop] += delta*a` double counts diag? a[diag]=1-2h0. Good. But if start:stop slice and then c[gdiag] modifies. Need not separately add delta for diag? We are adding for all including diag then subtract delta^2. correct.

Need ensure `a` at diag after adding both is exactly 1-2h0. Floating maybe. If diagonal term maybe both pos_add and pos_sub include diag, yes. If due orientation swapped, still both. Good.

Let's implement `solve_coord` carefully.
Pseudo:
```
def solve_coord(c, h, item):
    p,start,stop,pos_add,vals_add,pos_sub,vals_sub,diag_rel=item
    L=stop-start
    a=np.zeros(L,float)
    if pos_add.size:
        a[pos_add] += 1.0 - h[vals_add]
    if pos_sub.size:
        a[pos_sub] -= h[vals_sub]
    h0 = h[p]
    unaff=0.0
    if start > 0:
       unaff = float(np.max(c[:start]))
    rem = len(c)-stop
    if rem > 0:
       v = float(np.max(c[stop:]))
       if v>unaff: unaff=v
    cseg = c[start:stop]
    maxseg = float(np.max(cseg))
    Lhi = max(unaff, maxseg)
    tol=1e-14
    # line masks
    pos = a > tol
    neg = a < -tol
    if diag_rel >= 0:
       pos[diag_rel] = False
       neg[diag_rel] = False
    # zero max excluding diag
    zero = (a >= -tol) & (a <= tol)
    if diag_rel >= 0:
       zero[diag_rel] = False
    if np.any(zero):
       max_zero = float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
    else: max_zero = -1e300
    Llo = max(0.0, unaff, max_zero)
    if Lhi <= Llo + 1e-12:
       return h0, a
    cdiag = cseg[diag_rel]
    const_diag = cdiag - h0 + h0*h0
    pos_idx = np.nonzero(pos)[0]
    neg_idx = np.nonzero(neg)[0]
    # Precompute c/a? We can use cseg[pos_idx] each iter; creates temp. But okay.
    # Bisection
    for _ in range(32):
       Lmid = 0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       xl=0.0; xh=1.0; feasible=True
       if pos_idx.size:
           ap = a[pos_idx]
          cp = cseg[pos_idx]
           bounds = (Lmid - cp + ap*h0) / ap
           ub = float(np.min(bounds))
           if ub < xh: xh = ub
       if neg_idx.size:
           an = a[neg_idx]
           cn = cseg[neg_idx]
           bounds = (Lmid - cn + an*h0) / an
           lb = float(np.max(bounds))
           if lb > xl: xl = lb
       if xl > xh + 1e-12:
           feasible=False
       if feasible and diag_rel >=0:
           Cq = Lmid - const_diag
           D = 1.0 - 4.0*Cq
           if D >= 0.0:
              sq = np.sqrt(max(0.0,D))
              r1 = 0.5 - 0.5*sq
              r2 = 0.5 + 0.5*sq
              lo = xl if xl > 0.0 else 0.0
              hi = xh if xh < 1.0 else 1.0
              if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                  feasible=False
       if Lhi - Llo < 1e-10: break
       if feasible: Lhi = Lmid
       else: Llo = Lmid
    # final bounds at Lhi
    xl=0; xh=1
    if pos_idx.size:
       ... xh=min(xh, min_bound)
    if neg_idx.size:
       ...
    xl=max(xl,0); xh=min(xh,1)
    if xl <= xh:
       if diag_rel>=0:
          Cq=Lhi-const_diag; D=1-4*Cq
          if D>=0:
             sq=np.sqrt(max(0,D)); r1=0.5-0.5*sq; r2=0.5+0.5*sq
             left_hi = min(xh, r1)
             right_lo = max(xl, r2)
             # Determine feasible pieces
             if xl <= left_hi and right_lo <= xh:
                 # choose side closer to h0
                 midL = 0.5*(xl+left_hi)
                 midR = 0.5*(right_lo+xh)
                 if abs(midL-h0) <= abs(midR-h0):
                     xh = left_hi
                 else:
                     xl = right_lo
             elif xl <= left_hi:
                 xh = left_hi
             elif right_lo <= xh:
                 xl = right_lo
             else:
                 # no feasible due numerical; choose h0? But should not.
                 # Pick boundary outside forbidden closest to h0
                 cand = [0.0,1.0,r1,r2]
         # after maybe adjust
       if xl > xh:
           # fallback: choose projection of h0 to feasible via scan? Could just h0
           x = h0
       else:
           x = 0.5*(xl+xh)
    else:
       x = h0
    x = float(np.clip(x,0,1))
    return x, a
```

def choose_feasible_interval(xl,xh,r1,r2,h0):
    # interval [xl,xh] feasible by lines, forbidden [r1,r2] for diag. choose x.
    if xl > xh: return None

Implement helper:
```
def choose_feasible_interval(xl,xh,r1,r2,h0):
    eps=1e-12
    left_hi = r1 if r1 < xh else xh
    right_lo = r2 if r2 > xl else xl
    left_ok = xl <= left_hi + eps
    right_ok = xh >= right_lo - eps
    if not left_ok and not right_ok:
        # fallback choose 0/1 if feasible? Since at root line interval should intersect outside; maybe due eps.
        # choose point in [0,1] closest h0 outside [r1,r2]
        best = None
        for cand in (0.0, 1.0, r1, r2):
           if cand < -1e-9 or cand > 1+1e-9: continue
           if cand >= 0 and cand <=1 and not (r1+1e-10 < cand < r2-1e-10):
              d=abs(cand-h0)
              if best is None or d<bestd: best=cand; bestd=d
        if best is None: best = h0
        return float(np.clip(best,0,1))
    if left_ok and right_ok:
       midL=0.5*(xl+left_hi); midR=0.5*(right_lo+xh)
       if abs(midL-h0) <= abs(midR-h0):
          return float(np.clip(0.5*(xl+left_hi),0,1))
       else: return float(np.clip(0.5*(right_lo+xh),0,1))
    elif left_ok:
       return float(np.clip(0.5*(xl+left_hi),0,1))
    else:
       return float(np.clip(0.5*(right_lo+xh),0,1))

Run phases in detail:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  deadline=t0+budget_s-3
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # initial h
  try:
     h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
  except Exception:
     h0=np.full(700,0.5)
  n0=len(h0)
  h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
  best=h0.copy(); best_n=n0; best_c=compute_c5(h0,best_n)
  # 1. parent intensive
  # reserve for other: maybe 50%? Let's allocate up to 500s? budget 1100, target improve. We'll give 550 to parent? If CD very slow, can exceed. Use deadline_parent = min(t0+500, deadline-450)
  parent_deadline = min(t0 + 500.0, deadline - 450.0)
  if parent_deadline > t0+5:
     h,c=coordinate_descent(h0,n0,parent_deadline, 10000, rng, chunk=10)
     if c<best_c: best=... 
  # 2. alternate n from current best
  n_candidates = [best_n]
  # add resampled sizes near best and common: 500,600,700,800,900,1000,400
  extra=[400,500,600,800,900,1000]
  # avoid >1500
  for m in extra:
     if m==best_n: continue
     if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
     h_try=resample_h(best,m)
     # maybe small perturbation if m near? add tiny noise
     h_try=project_feasible(h_try + rng.normal(0,0.005,m), m)
     tr_limit = min(time.time()+80.0, deadline-120.0)
     if tr_limit <= time.time()+3: continue
     h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h_try,m,tr_limit,10000,rng,chunk=10)
     if c2 < best_c - 1e-10:
        best, best_n, best_c = h2,m,c2
  # 3. random starts
  random_ns = [500,700,900, best_n]
  for attempt in range(10):
     if time.time() > deadline-100: break
     m = random_ns[attempt % len(random_ns)]
     if attempt %3==0: h_try=random_smooth_start(m,rng)
     elif attempt %3==1: h_try=random_binary_start(m,rng)
     else:
        h_try=resample_h(best,m)
        h_try = np.where(h_try + 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,m) > 0.5, 1.0,0.0)? maybe binary push
        h_try=project_feasible(h_try,m)
     tr_limit = min(time.time()+60.0, deadline-80.0)
     if tr_limit <= time.time()+3: continue
     h2,c2=coordinate_descent(...)
     if c2 < best_c: update
  # 4. perturb best cycles
  # if time > deadline-80
  for sigma in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
     if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
     h_try=project_feasible(best + rng.normal(0,sigma,best_n), best_n)
     h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h_try,best_n,min(time.time()+80,deadline-50),10000,rng,chunk=10)
     if c2<best_c: update
  # 5. final intensive on best
  if time.time() < deadline - 10:
     h2,c2=coordinate_descent(best,best_n,deadline-2,10000,rng,chunk=10)
     if c2<best_c: update
  final=project_feasible(best,best_n)
  final_c=compute_c5(final,best_n)
  if final_c>best_c+1e-9: # maybe use saved evaluate? 
  return final, final_c, len(final)
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

_orient_cache = None

def compute_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full'))*2.0/n)
```

Project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h0=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0.0,1.0)
    tgt=0.5*n
    s0=float(h0.sum())
    if abs(s0-tgt)<1e-13:
        return h0
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(40):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if float(np.clip(h0+mid,0.0,1.0).sum()) < tgt:
            lo=mid
        else:
            hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h0+0.5*(lo+hi),0.0,1.0)
    diff=tgt-float(h.sum())
    if diff>1e-12:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff<=1e-13: break
            add=min(diff,1.0-h[i])
            if add>0:
                h[i]+=add; diff-=add
    elif diff<-1e-12:
        for i in range(n):
            if diff>=-1e-13: break
            sub=min(-diff,h[i])
            if sub>0:
                h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
    return h
```
This loops n for residual; if diff not fully corrected due many? It will correct in one pass if enough free; but if we add to first variables until saturated and diff remains, continues. Good.

Calibration:
```
def apply_delta_c(c,h,n,p,delta,orient):
    if delta == 0.0: return
    nm1=n-1; total=2*n-2
    start=min(p, nm1-p); stop=max(nm1+p, total-p)+1
    a=np.zeros(stop-start,float)
    d=np.arange(start,stop,dtype=np.int64)-nm1
    if orient==0:
        j=p-d; m=(j>=0)&(j<n); a[m]+=1.0-h[j[m]]
        j=p+d; m=(j>=0)&(j<n); a[m]-=h[j[m]]
    else:
        j=p+d; m=(j>=0)&(j<n); a[m]+=1.0-h[j[m]]
        j=p-d; m=(j>=0)&(j<n); a[m]-=h[j[m]]
    c[start:stop]+=delta*a
    c[start+nm1-start]-=delta*delta
```
Wait diag index global = n-1; relative = diag_rel = nm1 - start. Since start ≤ nm1 ≤ stop-1, yes.

```
def calibrate_orientation():
    m=7
    h=0.2+0.6*np.arange(m, dtype=float)/(m-1)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    p=1
    old=float(h[p]); new=old+0.01
    if new>1: new=0.9; delta=new-old
    else: delta=0.01
    h2=h.copy(); h2[p]=new
    true=np.correlate(h2,1.0-h2,mode='full')
    best=0; err=None
    for ori in (0,1):
        ct=c.copy()
        apply_delta_c(ct,h,m,p,delta,ori)
        e=float(np.max(np.abs(ct-true)))
        if err is None or e<err:
            err=e; best=ori
    return best
```
Need `if new>1: new=0.9; delta=new-old` but old maybe near1? h[1]=0.285, no. okay.
```
def get_orientation():
    global _orient_cache
    if _orient_cache is None:
        _orient_cache=calibrate_orientation()
    return _orient_cache
```
Potential: `apply_delta_c` uses h at old; if `new` clipped, delta correct. Good.

Build info:
```
def build_info(n,orient):
    info=[]; nm1=n-1; total=2*n-2
    for p in range(n):
        start=min(p,nm1-p)
        stop=max(nm1+p,total-p)+1
        d=np.arange(start,stop,dtype=np.int64)-nm1
        if orient==0:
            j1=p-d; mask1=(j1>=0)&(j1<n); pos1=np.nonzero(mask1)[0].astype(np.int32); val1=j1[mask1].astype(np.int32)
            j2=p+d; mask2=(j2>=0)&(j2<n); pos2=np.nonzero(mask2)[0].astype(np.int32); val2=j2[mask2].astype(np.int32)
        else:
            j1=p+d; mask1=(j1>=0)&(j1<n); pos1=...
            j2=p-d; mask2=...
        diag=nm1-start
        info.append((p,start,stop,pos1,val1,pos2,val2,diag))
    return info
```
Need if orient=0 add j1 subtract j2. In apply_delta, for orient0 add p-d subtract p+d. Good.

Choose feasible:
```
def choose_feasible_region(xl,xh,r1,r2,h0):
    eps=1e-12
    left_hi = r1 if r1 < xh else xh
    right_lo = r2 if r2 > xl else xl
    left_ok = xl <= left_hi + eps
    right_ok = xh >= right_lo - eps
    if left_ok and right_ok:
       a=0.5*(xl+left_hi); b=0.5*(right_lo+xh)
       if abs(a-h0) <= abs(b-h0): return float(np.clip(a,0,1))
       return float(np.clip(b,0,1))
    if left_ok: return float(np.clip(0.5*(xl+left_hi),0,1))
    if right_ok: return float(np.clip(0.5*(right_lo+xh),0,1))
    # fallback
    best=0.0; bestd=abs(best-h0)
    for cand in (1.0, r1, r2):
       if cand < -1e-8 or cand > 1+1e-8: continue
       val=float(np.clip(cand,0,1))
       if r1+1e-10 < val < r2-1e-10: continue
       d=abs(val-h0)
       if d<bestd: bestd=d; best=val
    return float(np.clip(best,0,1))
```
If left_ok true but left interval has negative width? left_hi < xl-eps? left_ok ensures. If left_hi is -0.1 and xl=0, false. Good. But if r1 outside lower, left_hi=xh? Wait if r1<xl, left_hi=r1? We did left_hi = r1 if r1<xh else xh. If r1<xl, left_hi=r1 <xl, left_ok false. But actual interval [xl,xh] may be entirely to right of r1, so should be considered right piece, not left. right_lo = r2 if r2>xl else xl. If r2 also <xl (forbidden left), right_lo=xl, right_ok true. Good. If r1>=xh (forbidden right), left_hi=xh, left_ok true (since xl<=xh). right_lo=r2 (since r2>xl), right_ok? xh>=r2 false. Good. So choose left. Good.

Solve coord:
```
def solve_coord(c,h,item):
    p,start,stop,pos_add,val_add,pos_sub,val_sub,diag=item
    L=stop-start
    a=np.zeros(L,float)
    if pos_add.size:
        a[pos_add] += 1.0 - h[val_add]
    if pos_sub.size:
        a[pos_sub] -= h[val_sub]
    h0=h[p]
    unaff=0.0
    if start>0:
        unaff=float(np.max(c[:start]))
    rem=len(c)-stop
    if rem>0:
        v=float(np.max(c[stop:]))
        if v>unaff: unaff=v
    cseg=c[start:stop]
    Lhi=max(unaff, float(np.max(cseg)))
    tol=1e-14
    pos=a > tol
    neg=a < -tol
    if diag>=0:
        pos[diag]=False; neg[diag]=False
    zero=(a <= tol) & (a >= -tol)
    if diag>=0: zero[diag]=False
    max_zero=-1e300
    if np.any(zero): max_zero=float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
    Llo=max(0.0, unaff, max_zero)
    if Lhi <= Llo + 1e-12:
        return float(h0), a
    cdiag=cseg[diag]
    const=cdiad - h0 + h0*h0
    pos_idx=np.nonzero(pos)[0]
    neg_idx=np.nonzero(neg)[0]
    # optional precompute arrays? To reduce overhead, assign local variables
    for _ in range(30):
       Lm=0.5*(Llo+Lhi)
       xl=0.0; xh=1.0; feas=True
       if pos_idx.size:
          ap=a[pos_idx]
          ub=float(np.min((Lm - cseg[pos_idx] + ap*h0)/ap))
          if ub < xh: xh=ub
       if neg_idx.size:
          an=a[neg_idx]
          lb=float(np.max((Lm - cseg[neg_idx] + an*h0)/an))
          if lb > xl: xl=lb
       if xl > xh + 1e-12: feas=False
       if feas:
          Cq=Lm-const
          D=1.0-4.0*Cq
          if D>=0.0:
             sq=np.sqrt(D)
             r1=0.5-0.5*sq; r2=0.5+0.5*sq
             lo=xl if xl>0 else 0.0
             hi=xh if xh<1.0 else 1.0
             if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                 feas=False
       if Lhi-Llo<1e-10: break
       if feas: Lhi=Lm
       else: Llo=Lm
    xl=0.0; xh=1.0
    if pos_idx.size:
       ap=a[pos_idx]
       ub=float(np.min((Lhi - cseg[pos_idx] + ap*h0)/ap))
       if ub < xh: xh=ub
    if neg_idx.size:
       an=a[neg_idx]
       lb=float(np.max((Lhi - cseg[neg_idx] + an*h0)/an))
       if lb > xl: xl=lb
    xl=max(xl,0.0); xh=min(xh,1.0)
    if xl > xh + 1e-12:
       x=float(h0)
    else:
       Cq=Lhi-const
       D=1.0-4.0*Cq
       if D>=0.0:
          sq=np.sqrt(max(0.0,D)); r1=0.5-0.5*sq; r2=0.5+0.5*sq
          x=choose_feasible_region(xl,xh,r1,r2,h0)
       else:
          x=0.5*(xl+xh)
    return float(np.clip(x,0,1)), a
```
Need in bisection diag: If pos/neg constraints produced xl,xh out of [0,1], `lo=max(xl,0)`, `hi=min(xh,1)`. If lo>hi? E.g., xl>xh already handled. If one bound >1, hi=1; if lo maybe >1? then lo>hi maybe but xh could >1? In line constraints bounds could be >1 or <0; xl,xh not clamped until after. For diag feasibility, [lo,hi] may be empty if xl>xh handled; or if xl<0, xh<0? Then hi=xh<0, lo=0, lo>hi. But xh<0 means no feasible x in [0,1] from lines? However xh initialized 1 and only min with ub; if ub<0, xh<0, and xl=0, xl > xh+eps => infeasible. So not. If xl<0? initialized 0 and max with lb; if lb<0, xl stays0. So xl>=0, xh<=1? xh initialized1, min with ub; if ub>1, stays1. So actually xl∈[0,?], xh∈[?,1]. But line ub could >1, xh=1; lb<0 xl=0. Thus clipped inside loop already. Good. No need lo/hi but use.

In final bounds, `ub` could nan? no. If `ap` signs, (Lhi - c + ap*h0)/ap. Good.

Potential optimization: In bisection, `cseg[pos_idx]` and `a[pos_idx]` repeated. Could precompute:
```
cp = cseg[pos_idx]; ap=a[pos_idx]
cn = cseg[neg_idx]; an=a[neg_idx]
```
after pos_idx. Use in loop. This saves advanced indexing each iter. Let's modify:
```
if pos_idx.size: cp=...; ap=... else None
if neg_idx.size: cn=...; an=...
for:
  if pos_idx.size: ub=min((Lm - cp + ap*h0)/ap)
```
Use arrays cp,ap fixed. Good.

Also zero mask scanning: `zero = (a <= tol) & (a >= -tol)` creates bool. Could instead `zero = np.abs(a) <= tol`; same. Use one.

Coordinate descent:
```
def coordinate_descent(h,n,deadline,max_passes,rng,chunk=10,noise_tries=4):
    orient=get_orientation()
    info=build_info(n,orient)
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    idx=np.arange(n)
    noise_left=noise_tries
    no_improve=0
    last_best=best_c
    for it in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline + 1e-9: break
        rng.shuffle(idx)
        changed=0; since=0
        for p in idx:
            if time.time() > deadline + 1e-9: break
            item=info[p]
            x,a=solve_coord(c,h,item)
            delta=x-h[p]
            if abs(delta)>1e-13:
                start=item[1]; stop=item[2]; diag=item[7]
                c[start:stop] += delta*a
                c[start+diag] -= delta*delta
                h[p]=x
                changed +=1; since +=1
                if since >= chunk:
                    h=project_feasible(h,n)
                    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                    since=0
                    cur=compute_c5(h,n)
                    if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
        if time.time() <= deadline + 1e-9 and since>0:
            h=project_feasible(h,n)
            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
        if since>0 or changed:
            cur=compute_c5(h,n) if len(h)>0 else best_c
            if cur<best_c-1e-12:
                best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy(); no_improve=0
            else:
                no_improve +=1
        if changed==0:
            if noise_left>0 and no_improve>=0:
                sigma=0.015+0.02*(noise_tries-noise_left)
                hp=project_feasible(h+rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n),n)
                h=hp; c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
                noise_left-=1
                continue
            break
        if no_improve>4 and noise_left>0:
            sigma=0.02+0.02*(noise_tries-noise_left)
            h=project_feasible(h+rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n),n)
            c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
            noise_left-=1
            no_improve=0
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cur=compute_c5(h,n)
    if cur<best_c:
        best_c=cur; best_h=h.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
Issue: `since>0 or changed` after break due deadline: if inner loop broke due deadline, since might be >0 but we need project. The code `if time.time() <= deadline and since>0` maybe not; even if past deadline, we should project for final. Just do if since>0: project. Then compute cur if feasible? But if deadline expired, compute okay. Good.
If inner loop broke mid-coordinate (after solve but before update? The break only at top before solve; if past during solve maybe no state). Since count matches.
The `changed==0` branch: If no changes but noise_left>0, we perturb immediately. Could perturb even if first pass no changes due local. Good. But if noise_left used, continue. If no changes and noise_left=0, break.
If changes but no_improve>4, perturb. Good.
However coordinate_descent's `best_h` is feasible after chunks. During pass after projection but before chunk end? best after each chunk. Good.

Potential performance of project_feasible every 10 coordinate updates: For n=700, 70 per pass, maybe okay. But after projection, we recompute c. We also at end of pass project. If `since` exactly multiple chunk, since=0 no end project. Good.

Need ensure h sum exactly after final. `project_feasible` returns sum target within tolerance. Good.

Now `resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,m):
    if m == h.size: return h.copy()
    xo=(np.arange(h.size)+0.5)*2.0/h.size
    xn=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn,xo,h),m)
```
If input h has noise? fine.

`random_start(n,rng,kind)`:
```
def random_start(n,rng,kind):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    if kind==0:
       h=0.5*np.ones(n)
       for _ in range(3):
          f=int(rng.randint(1,5))
          a=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.25))
          ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          if rng.rand()<0.5: h += a*np.sin(np.pi*f*x+ph)
          else: h += a*np.cos(np.pi*f*x+ph)
       h += rng.normal(0,0.04,n)
       return project_feasible(h,n)
    if kind==1:
       h=np.zeros(n)
       order=np.argsort(rng.random(n))
       if n%2==0:
           h[order[n//2:]] = 1.0
       else:
           h[order[(n+1)//2:]] = 1.0
           h[order[(n-1)//2]] = 0.5
       return project_feasible(h,n)
    # kind 2 threshold current? Can't pass current inside. We'll do in run.
    return project_feasible(0.5*np.ones(n),n)
```
For odd binary: n=5, target 2.5. `(n+1)//2=3`: h[order[3:]] top2 ones sum2; h[order[2]]=0.5 total2.5. Good. For even n=4 target2: order[2:] top2 ones. Good.

`binary_from(h,rng)`:
```
def binary_from(h,rng):
    x=h+rng.normal(0,0.1,h.size)
    order=np.argsort(x)
    res=np.zeros(h.size)
    n=h.size
    if n%2==0:
       res[order[n//2:]] = 1.0
    else:
       res[order[(n+1)//2:]] = 1.0
       res[order[(n-1)//2]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(res,n)
```
Use in run.

Run:

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-3.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h_initial=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
       h_initial=0.5*np.full(700)
    n_initial=len(h_initial)
    h_initial=project_feasible(h_initial,n_initial)
    best=h_initial.copy(); best_n=n_initial; best_c=compute_c5(best,best_n)
    # parent
    parent_dl=min(t0+500.0, deadline-450.0)
    if parent_dl > t0+5.0:
       h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h_initial,best_n,parent_dl,10000,rng,chunk=10,noise_tries=4)
       if c2<best_c:
          best=h2; best_n=len(h2); best_c=c2
    # alt n
    alt_sizes=[400,500,600,800,900,1000]
    for m in alt_sizes:
       if time.time() > deadline-140.0: break
       if m==best_n: continue
       h_try=resample_h(best,m)
       h_try=project_feasible(h_try + rng.normal(0,0.003,m),m)
       dl=min(time.time()+90.0, deadline-140.0)
       if dl <= time.time()+5.0: continue
       h2,c2=coordinate_descent(h_try,m,dl,10000,rng,chunk=10,noise_tries=3)
       if c2<best_c-1e-10:
          best=h2; best_n=m; best_c=c2
    # random/binary starts
    random_modes=[0,1,2]
    for k in range(8):
       if time.time() > deadline-110.0: break
       m=choice? Need no lambda. Use list sizes.
```

          h_try=random_start(m,rng,0)

Implement `project_feasible` concisely:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1).copy()
    tgt=0.5*float(n)
    s=float(h.sum())
    if abs(s-tgt)<1e-13: return h
    lo,hi=-1.0,1.0
    for _ in range(50):
        mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
        if float(np.clip(h+mid,0,1).sum()) < tgt: lo=mid
        else: hi=mid
    h=np.clip(h+0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff=tgt-float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            for i in range(n):
                if diff<=1e-13: break
                add=min(diff,1.0-h[i])
                if add>0:
                    h[i]+=add; diff-=add
        else:
            for i in range(n):
                if diff>=-1e-13: break
                sub=min(-diff,h[i])
                if sub>0:
                    h[i]-=sub; diff+=sub
    return h
```
Good.

def c5_from_c(c,n):

def active_subgrad_step(h,n,c,step):

```
def calibrate_orientation():
    m=7
    x=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
    h=0.2 + 0.6*x/(m-1)
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    p=1
    old=float(h[p])
    new=old+0.01
    if new>1.0:
       new=0.9; delta=new-old
    else:
       delta=0.01
    h2=h.copy(); h2[p]=new
    true=np.correlate(h2,1.0-h2,mode='full')
    best_ori=0; best_err=1e30
    for ori in (0,1):
       ct=c.copy()
       apply_delta_c(ct,h,m,p,delta,ori)
       err=float(np.max(np.abs(ct-true)))
       if err<best_err:
          best_err=err; best_ori=ori
    return best_ori
```

For n=3 top1 (index2) + idx1 0.5 sum1.5 target1.5. Good. For even n=4: (n+1)//2? not used. even branch `res[order[n//2:]] =1` top2 sum2 target2. Good.
`binary_from` same.

In `random_start` kind 0, if using cos, integral zero? cos(π f x) over 0..2 integer f has zero. Constant 0.5 plus noise, then project. Good.

One concern: `project_feasible` exact residual after binary search may break monotonic if h has nan? no.

Now, let's mentally test small behavior of solve_coord with no line pos/neg (all a zero). Then Llo=unaff (unaff maybe max outside), Lhi=max(unaff,maxseg). If maxseg >unaff due zero? But if all a zero, changing x doesn't change cseg? Except diag? Diag excluded from lines but a at diag zero? At h0=0.5, a diag 0; pos/neg false, zero includes diag? We exclude diag, zero other lines. max_zero maybe cseg non-diag. Llo=max(unaff,max_zero). Lhi=max(unaff,maxseg)=max(unaff,max_zero, cdiag if diag not included) maybe cdiag larger. Bisection: line constraints none, diag constraint. Llo maybe less than cdiag; Lhi=cd. Feasibility diag. It will find x to reduce diag. Good.
If all a zero including diag? h0=0.5, cdiag maybe max. Good.

But in solve, `zero_mask` excludes diag; max_zero may be less than cdiag. If Llo < minimal diag, bisection works.

In final choose when D>=0, if line interval [0,1] and forbidden middle, choose side closer to h0. If h0=0.5, left/right mids .25/.75 equal, choose left (<=). Good. Moving to 0.25 reduces diag.

Now, one bug: In bisection feasibility diag, we use `if D >= 0.0` forbidden interval. But if L is very low such that Cq large, D negative? Wait f=-x^2+x+const. f≤L. If L is very low, Cq=L-const very negative? Example const=0, L=-10, Cq=-10, D=1+40=41 positive, roots complex? Actually if L < min possible -? f min at endpoints 0, cannot satisfy L negative, but D=41 positive roots (0.5 ± sqrt41/2) outside [0,1], forbidden interval covers [0,1]? r1 negative, r2>1, lo=0>r1 true, hi=1<r2 true => infeasible. Good. If L=0.1, Cq=0.1, D=0.6 positive, roots small and near1, forbidden middle; feasible near endpoints. Good. If L=0.25, D=0 roots 0.5, forbidden point; feasible elsewhere. If L=0.3, Cq=0.3, D=-0.2, always true (f max 0.25). Good. So D logic correct.

Now, in final choose if D>=0 and roots maybe r1>r2? sqrt nonnegative. Good.

Potential performance issue: In `solve_coord`, `np.any(zero)` and `np.max(cseg[zero])` each coordinate. We can avoid if no zero by checking `zero.any()`. okay.

Now, if `pos_add.size` is 0? For n? p? It should be >0. But if n=0? no.

Now, we should not import scipy? Not needed. Fine.

Let's ensure no line too long? Not critical.

Now produce final code. Need only one fenced block. Let's include code.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_orient_cache = None
_info_cache = {}


def c5_from_c(c, n):
    v = float(np.max(c))
    if v < 0.0:
        v = 0.0
    return v * (2.0 / float(n))


def compute_c5(h, n):
    return c5_from_c(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full'), n)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    tgt = 0.5 * float(n)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - tgt) < 1e-13:
        return h
    lo, hi = -1.0, 1.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if float(np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()) < tgt:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    h = np.clip(h + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(5):
        diff = tgt - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            for i in range(n):
                if diff <= 1e-13:
                    break
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[i])
                if add > 0.0:
                    h[i] += add
                    diff -= add
        else:
            for i in range(n):
                if diff >= -1e-13:
                    break
                sub = min(-diff, h[i])
                if sub > 0.0:
                    h[i] -= sub
                    diff += sub
    return h


def apply_delta_c(c, h, n, p, delta, orient):
    if delta == 0.0:
        return
    nm1 = n - 1
    total = 2 * n - 2
    start = min(p, nm1 - p)
    stop = max(nm1 + p, total - p) + 1
    a = np.zeros(stop - start, float)
    d = np.arange(start, stop, dtype=np.int64) - nm1
    if orient == 0:
        j = p - d
        m = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
        a[m] += 1.0 - h[j[m]]
        j = p + d
        m = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
        a[m] -= h[j[m]]
    else:
        j = p + d
        m = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
        a[m] += 1.0 - h[j[m]]
        j = p - d
        m = (j >= 0) & (j < n)
        a[m] -= h[j[m]]
    c[start:stop] += delta * a
    c[start + nm1 - start] -= delta * delta


def calibrate_orientation():
    m = 7
    h = 0.2 + 0.6 * np.arange(m, dtype=float) / float(m - 1)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    p = 1
    old = float(h[p])
    new = old + 0.01
    if new > 1.0:
        new = 0.9
    delta = new - old
    h2 = h.copy()
    h2[p] = new
    true = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode='full')
    best_ori = 0
    best_err = 1e100
    for ori in (0, 1):
        ct = c.copy()
        apply_delta_c(ct, h, m, p, delta, ori)
        err = float(np.max(np.abs(ct - true)))
        if err < best_err:
            best_err = err
            best_ori = ori
    return best_ori


def get_orientation():
    global _orient_cache
    if _orient_cache is None:
        _orient_cache = calibrate_orientation()
    return _orient_cache


def build_info(n, orient):
    info = []
    nm1 = n - 1
    total = 2 * n - 2
    for p in range(n):
        start = min(p, nm1 - p)
        stop = max(nm1 + p, total - p) + 1
        d = np.arange(start, stop, dtype=np.int64) - nm1
        if orient == 0:
            j1 = p - d
            m1 = (j1 >= 0) & (j1 < n)
            pos1 = np.nonzero(m1)[0].astype(np.int32)
            val1 = j1[m1].astype(np.int32)
            j2 = p + d
            m2 = (j2 >= 0) & (j2 < n)
            pos2 = np.nonzero(m2)[0].astype(np.int32)
            val2 = j2[m2].astype(np.int32)
        else:
            j1 = p + d
            m1 = (j1 >= 0) & (j1 < n)
            pos1 = np.nonzero(m1)[0].astype(np.int32)
            val1 = j1[m1].astype(np.int32)
            j2 = p - d
            m2 = (j2 >= 0) & (j2 < n)
            pos2 = np.nonzero(m2)[0].astype(np.int32)
            val2 = j2[m2].astype(np.int32)
        diag = nm1 - start
        info.append((p, start, stop, pos1, val1, pos2, val2, diag))
    return info


def get_info(n, orient):
    key = (n, orient)
    if key in _info_cache:
        return _info_cache[key]
    if len(_info_cache) >= 3:
        _info_cache.clear()
    _info_cache[key] = build_info(n, orient)
    return _info_cache[key]


def choose_feasible_region(xl, xh, r1, r2, h0):
    eps = 1e-12
    left_hi = r1 if r1 < xh else xh
    right_lo = r2 if r2 > xl else xl
    left_ok = xl <= left_hi + eps
    right_ok = xh >= right_lo - eps
    if left_ok and right_ok:
        a = 0.5 * (xl + left_hi)
        b = 0.5 * (right_lo + xh)
        if abs(a - h0) <= abs(b - h0):
            return float(np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0))
        return float(np.clip(b, 0.0, 1.0))
    if left_ok:
        return float(np.clip(0.5 * (xl + left_hi), 0.0, 1.0))
    if right_ok:
        return float(np.clip(0.5 * (right_lo + xh), 0.0, 1.0))
    best = 0.0
    bestd = 1e100
    for cand in (0.0, 1.0, r1, r2):
        val = float(np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0))
        if r1 + 1e-10 < val < r2 - 1e-10:
            continue
        dd = abs(val - h0)
        if dd < bestd:
            bestd = dd
            best = val
    return best


def solve_coord(c, h, item):
    p, start, stop, pos_add, val_add, pos_sub, val_sub, diag = item
    L = stop - start
    a = np.zeros(L, float)
    if pos_add.size:
        a[pos_add] += 1.0 - h[val_add]
    if pos_sub.size:
        a[pos_sub] -= h[val_sub]
    h0 = float(h[p])
    unaff = 0.0
    if start > 0:
        unaff = float(np.max(c[:start]))
    rem = len(c) - stop
    if rem > 0:
        v = float(np.max(c[stop:]))
        if v > unaff:
            unaff = v
    cseg = c[start:stop]
    Lhi = max(unaff, float(np.max(cseg)))
    tol = 1e-14
    pos = a > tol
    neg = a < -tol
    pos[diag] = False
    neg[diag] = False
    zero = (a <= tol) & (a >= -tol)
    zero[diag] = False
    max_zero = -1e100
    if np.any(zero):
        max_zero = float(np.max(cseg[zero]))
    Llo = max(0.0, unaff, max_zero)
    if Lhi <= Llo + 1e-12:
        return h0, a
    cdiag = float(cseg[diag])
    const = cdiag - h0 + h0 * h0
    pos_idx = np.nonzero(pos)[0]
    neg_idx = np.nonzero(neg)[0]
    if pos_idx.size:
        cp = cseg[pos_idx]
        ap = a[pos_idx]
    else:
        cp = None
        ap = None
    if neg_idx.size:
        cn = cseg[neg_idx]
        an = a[neg_idx]
    else:
        cn = None
        an = None
    for _ in range(32):
        Lm = 0.5 * (Llo + Lhi)
        xl = 0.0
        xh = 1.0
        feas = True
        if pos_idx.size:
            ub = float(np.min((Lm - cp + ap * h0) / ap))
            if ub < xh:
                xh = ub
        if neg_idx.size:
            lb = float(np.max((Lm - cn + an * h0) / an))
            if lb > xl:
                xl = lb
        if xl > xh + 1e-12:
            feas = False
        if feas:
            lo = max(xl, 0.0)
            hi = min(xh, 1.0)
            if lo <= hi + 1e-12:
                Cq = Lm - const
                D = 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq
                if D >= 0.0:
                    sq = float(np.sqrt(D))
                    r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * sq
                    r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * sq
                    if lo > r1 + 1e-12 and hi < r2 - 1e-12:
                        feas = False
        if Lhi - Llo < 1e-10:
            break
        if feas:
            Lhi = Lm
        else:
            Llo = Lm
    xl = 0.0
    xh = 1.0
    if pos_idx.size:
        ub = float(np.min((Lhi - cp + ap * h0) / ap))
        if ub < xh:
            xh = ub
    if neg_idx.size:
        lb = float(np.max((Lhi - cn + an * h0) / an))
        if lb > xl:
            xl = lb
    xl = max(xl, 0.0)
    xh = min(xh, 1.0)
    if xl > xh + 1e-12:
        x = h0
    else:
        Cq = Lhi - const
        D = 1.0 - 4.0 * Cq
        if D >= 0.0:
            sq = float(np.sqrt(max(0.0, D)))
            r1 = 0.5 - 0.5 * sq
            r2 = 0.5 + 0.5 * sq
            x = choose_feasible_region(xl, xh, r1, r2, h0)
        else:
            x = 0.5 * (xl + xh)
    return float(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)), a


def coordinate_descent(h, n, deadline, max_passes, rng, noise_tries=4, chunk=None):
    orient = get_orientation()
    info = get_info(n, orient)
    if chunk is None:
        chunk = 8 if n <= 800 else 14
    h = project_feasible(h, n).copy()
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    best_c = c5_from_c(c, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    idx = np.arange(n)
    left_noise = int(noise_tries)
    no_improve = 0
    for _ in range(int(max_passes)):
        if time.time() > deadline + 1e-9:
            break
        rng.shuffle(idx)
        changed = 0
        since = 0
        for p in idx:
            if time.time() > deadline + 1e-9:
                break
            item = info[p]
            x, a = solve_coord(c, h, item)
            delta = x - h[p]
            if abs(delta) > 1e-13:
                start = item[1]
                stop = item[2]
                c[start:stop] += delta * a
                c[start + item[7]] -= delta * delta
                h[p] = x
                changed += 1
                since += 1
                if since >= chunk:
                    h = project_feasible(h, n)
                    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                    since = 0
                    cur = c5_from_c(c, n)
                    if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
                        best_c = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
        if since > 0:
            h = project_feasible(h, n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
        cur = c5_from_c(c, n)
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cur
            best_h = h.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        if changed == 0:
            if left_noise > 0:
                sigma = 0.015 + 0.02 * float(noise_tries - left_noise)
                h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), n)
                c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
                left_noise -= 1
                continue
            break
        if no_improve > 4 and left_noise > 0:
            sigma = 0.02 + 0.02 * float(noise_tries - left_noise)
            h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n), n)
            c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
            left_noise -= 1
            no_improve = 0
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
    cur = c5_from_c(c, n)
    if cur < best_c:
        best_c = cur
        best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def resample_h(h, m):
    m = int(m)
    if m == h.size:
        return h.copy()
    xo = (np.arange(h.size) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(h.size))
    xn = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(m))
    return project_feasible(np.interp(xn, xo, h), m)


def random_start(n, rng, kind):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(n))
    if kind == 0:
        h = 0.5 * np.ones(n)
        for _ in range(3):
            f = int(rng.randint(1, 5))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.06, 0.24))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                h += amp * np.sin(np.pi * f * x + ph)
            else:
                h += amp * np.cos(np.pi * f * x + ph)
        h += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    h = np.zeros(n)
    order = np.argsort(rng.random(n))
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h[order[n // 2:]] = 1.0
    else:
        h[order[(n + 1) // 2:]] = 1.0
        h[order[(n - 1) // 2]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def binary_from(h, rng):
    n = h.size
    x = h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, n)
    order = np.argsort(x)
    h2 = np.zeros(n)
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h2[order[n // 2:]] = 1.0
    else:
        h2[order[(n + 1) // 2:]] = 1.0
        h2[order[(n - 1) // 2]] = 0.5
    return project_feasible(h2, n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    _info_cache.clear()
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 3.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        h0 = random_start(700, rng, 0)
    n0 = int(h0.size)
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)
    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = n0
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    parent_dl = min(t0 + 500.0, deadline - 450.0)
    if parent_dl > t0 + 5.0:
        h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h0, best_n, parent_dl, 100000, rng, 5)
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2

    alt_sizes = (400, 500, 600, 800, 900, 1000)
    for m in alt_sizes:
        if time.time() >= deadline - 180.0:
            break
        m = int(m)
        if m == best_n or m < 100:
            continue
        try:
            h_try = resample_h(best_h, m)
            h_try = project_feasible(h_try + rng.normal(0.0, 0.004, m), m)
            dl = min(time.time() + 60.0, deadline - 180.0)
            if dl <= time.time() + 5.0:
                continue
            h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h_try, m, dl, 100000, rng, 3)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-10:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    for k in range(8):
        if time.time() >= deadline - 90.0:
            break
        m = int((best_n, 500, 900, 700)[k % 4])
        if m < 100:
            m = 500
        try:
            if k % 3 == 0:
                h_try = random_start(m, rng, 0)
            elif k % 3 == 1:
                h_try = random_start(m, rng, 1)
            else:
                h_try = resample_h(best_h, m)
                h_try = binary_from(h_try, rng)
            dl = min(time.time() + 50.0, deadline - 90.0)
            if dl <= time.time() + 5.0:
                continue
            h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h_try, m, dl, 100000, rng, 2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-10:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 60.0:
        try:
            h_try = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, best_n), best_n)
            dl = min(time.time() + 60.0, deadline - 50.0)
            if dl > time.time() + 5.0:
                h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(h_try, best_n, dl, 100000, rng, 2)
                if c2 < best_c - 1e-10:
                    best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        try:
            h2, c2 = coordinate_descent(best_h, best_n, deadline - 2.0, 100000, rng, 3)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h, best_n, best_c = h2, int(h2.size), c2
        except Exception:
            pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_h.size)
```
